## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner on this Mac. For example, I can open a task to fix a failing test in one of your repos and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, y **merge** cuando la rama ya es pública o tiene trabajo de otras personas. Nunca hagas rebase de ramas que otros ya usan, porque reescribe su historia.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的面向连接传输：数据有确认、丢了会重传、按序交付；UDP 则不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours alone and you want a clean, linear history, such as tidying up your commits before a PR or pulling the latest main into your feature branch. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, because rebasing rewrites commits others may have built on.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change is written down before it is applied to the actual data. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart so committed changes survive and half-done ones can be cleaned up.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着那个 compaction 的 PR 把锁粒度改细，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 按 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把 compaction 过程里的锁改细：只锁真正需要互斥的部分，不要整个 compaction 期间都持有大锁。
- 改动留在这个任务自己的分支上，提交到该分支。
- 跑相关测试，把结果作为证据报告（测试输出、改动的文件和要点）。
- 完成的标准：锁的范围缩小后，原有测试仍然通过，并说明每把锁现在保护什么。

不做的事：
- 不合并到主分支。
- 不推送到远端、不更新或评论 PR，等用户说 Go 再做。

地方：这个任务原来的仓库和分支。
```

  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2446 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 11/27 北京南 → 上海虹桥 · 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt"><span class="k-none">还没看</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"><div class="k-empty">还没有班次数据</div></div>
</div>
<style>
.k-app{padding:16px;background:var(--d-bg);color:var(--d-ink);font-family:inherit}
.k-sum-label{color:var(--d-muted);font-size:13px}
.k-sum-value{font-size:32px;font-weight:600;margin:4px 0}
.k-sum-sub{color:var(--d-soft);font-size:13px}
.k-none{color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-h{font-size:15px;margin:18px 0 8px}
.k-card{background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:12px;padding:4px 14px}
.k-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:12px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--d-line)}
.k-row:last-child{border-bottom:0}
.k-row-main{font-weight:600}
.k-row-sub{color:var(--d-muted);font-size:12px;margin-top:2px}
.k-row-value{text-align:right;font-weight:600}
.k-row-value.k-bad{color:var(--d-muted);font-weight:400}
.k-row-value.k-good{color:var(--d-good)}
.k-empty{color:var(--d-muted);padding:12px 0}
</style>
<script>
function render(d){
  var list = (d && d.trains) || [];
  if (!list.length) return;
  var cheap = list.filter(function(t){ return t.price < 600; });
  var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  var box = document.getElementById('list');
  cnt.textContent = cheap.length + ' 班';
  if (cheap.length) {
    var min = Math.min.apply(null, cheap.map(function(t){ return t.price; }));
    var low = cheap.filter(function(t){ return t.left != null && t.left <= 5; });
    sub.textContent = '最低 ¥' + min + (low.length ? ' · ' + low.map(function(t){ return t.no + ' 只剩 ' + t.left + ' 张'; }).join('，') : '');
  } else {
    sub.textContent = '今天没有 600 元以下的';
  }
  box.innerHTML = list.map(function(t){
    var ok = t.price < 600;
    var left = t.left != null ? '二等座余 ' + t.left + ' 张' : '';
    return '<div class="k-row"><div><div class="k-row-main">' + t.no + '</div><div class="k-row-sub">' + left + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value ' + (ok ? 'k-good' : 'k-bad') + '">¥' + t.price + '</div></div>';
  }).join('');
}
var v = ducter.get('trains');
if (v && typeof v.then === 'function') v.then(render); else render(v);
ducter.onChange(render);
</script>

  body { background: var(--d-bg); color: var(--d-ink); font-family: -apple-system, system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0; }

        '<div class="k-row-value">' + (r.wps / 1000).toFixed(0) + 'k/s</div>';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

document.getElementById('days').addEventListener('click', function (e) {

  if (!row) return;
  var id = Number(row.getAttribute('data-id'));
  var it = items.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0];
  if (!it) return;
  Promise.resolve(ducter.ask('删掉「' + (it.what || '未命名') + ' ¥' + fmt(it.amount) + '」?')).then(function (a) {
    var s = String(a || '');
    if (/删|yes|go|是|好/i.test(s) && !/不|no|否|取消|hold/i.test(s)) {
      items = items.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== id; });
      ducter.set('expenses', items);
      render();
    }
  });
});

function yuan(n){return "¥"+Number(n||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const sum=xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=yuan(sum);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=yuan(x.yuan);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>

</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var KEY = 'expenses';
  var items = [];
  var selected = -1;

  function money(n) {
    return '¥' + Number(n).toLocaleString('zh-CN', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
  }

  function total() {
    return items.reduce(function (s, it) { return s + Number(it.amount || 0); }, 0);
  }

  function … ment.getElementById('count').textContent = items.length ? items.length + ' 笔' : '还没记';
    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) {
      ducter.summary(items.length ? items.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + money(t) : '还没记');
    }
  }

  function save() {
    if (window.ducter && ducter.set) ducter.set(KEY, items);
    render();
  }

  function load(v) {
    items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  }

  document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('amt').value);
    if (!(amount > 0)) return;
    items.push({
      what: document.getElementById('what').value.trim() || '未命名',
      amount: amount,
      day: document.getElementById('day').value.trim(),
      from: ''
    });
    document.getElementById('what').value = '';
    document.getElementById('amt').value = '';
    document.getElementById('day').value = '';
    document.querySelector('.k-add').open = false;
    save();
  });

  if (window.ducter) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load);
    if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function () {
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load);
    });
  } else {
    render();
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南","amount":662,"day":"11/20","from":"你的预订"},{"what":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","amount":1036,"day":"11/20–21","from":"你的预订"}]}

<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">今天喝了</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="tot"></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">今天的记录</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<details class="k-add" id="add">
  <summary id="addTitle">记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>时间(如 06:10)</label><input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="06:10"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="120"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
  <button class="k-btn" id="del" style="display:none">删除这条</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const KEY = 'feeds';
let editing = null;
function ymd(d){return d.getFullYear()+'-'+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');}
function hm(d){return String(d.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');}
const today = ymd(new Date());
function all(){ return ducter.get(KEY) || []; }
function esc(s){ return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c])); }

function render(){
  const items = all().map(( … tElementById('t').value = hm(new Date());
  document.getElementById('ml').value = '';
  document.getElementById('addTitle').textContent = '记一笔';
  document.getElementById('save').textContent = '记下';
  document.getElementById('del').style.display = 'none';
}

document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (row) openEdit(Number(row.dataset.i));
});

document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const t = document.getElementById('t').value.trim();
  const ml = Number(document.getElementById('ml').value);
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  const arr = all().slice();
  if (editing !== null) arr[editing] = { d: arr[editing].d, t, ml };
  else arr.push({ d: today, t, ml });
  ducter.set(KEY, arr);
  resetForm();
  document.getElementById('add').open = false;
  render();
});

document.getElementById('del').addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (editing === null) return;
  const arr = all().slice();
  arr.splice(editing, 1);
  ducter.set(KEY, arr);
  resetForm();
  document.getElementById('add').open = false;
  render();
});

resetForm();
render();
ducter.onChange(render);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-26","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-26","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: "PRs waiting on someone" is on your shelf, with the four PRs. Tap a row when it lands and the count goes down.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1921 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">tap a row when it lands</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
  <section class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </section>
</main>
<script>
var DEFAULT = ducter.get("default") || [];
var state = DEFAULT;

function save() { ducter.set("prs", JSON.stringify(state)); }

function render() {
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  var left = 0;
  state.forEach(function (p) {
    if (!p.done) left++;
    var li = document.createElement("li");
    li.className = "k-row";
    var btn = document.createElement("button");
    btn.className = "k-chip";
    btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", p.done ? "true" : "false");
    btn.textContent = p.done ? "✓" : "";
    btn.onclick = function () { p.done = !p.done; save(); render(); };
    var main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = p.repo + " · " + p.title;
    var sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = p.done ? "landed" : p.waiting;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    li.appendChild(btn);
    li.appendChild(main);
    list.appendChild(li);
  });
  document.getElementById("left").textContent = String(left);
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent = left === 0 ? "all landed" : "tap a row when it lands";
  ducter.summary(left + " waiting");
}

Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(function (v) {
  if (v) {
    try { state = typeof v === "string" ? JSON.parse(v) : v; } catch (e) { state = DEFAULT; }
  }
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(function () {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(function (v) {
    if (v) { try { state = typeof v === "string" ? JSON.parse(v) : v; } catch (e) {} render(); }
  });
});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":"[{\"id\":\"ducter-412\",\"repo\":\"wey-gu/ducter#412\",\"title\":\"Compaction cut\",\"waiting\":\"Weizhen's review, since Monday\",\"done\":false},{\"id\":\"mem-10287\",\"repo\":\"nowledge-co/mem#10287\",\"title\":\"Retry on 502\",\"waiting\":\"CI, failing\",\"done\":false},{\"id\":\"hawdb-1592\",\"repo\":\"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592\",\"title\":\"Flaky compaction test\",\"waiting\":\"Alice, since Tuesday\",\"done\":false},{\"id\":\"ducter-418\",\"repo\":\"wey-gu/ducter#418\",\"title\":\"Apps bar\",\"waiting\":\"Draft\",\"done\":false}]","default":[{"id":"ducter-412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","waiting":"Weizhen's review, since Monday","done":false},{"id":"mem-10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","waiting":"CI, failing","done":false},{"id":"hawdb-1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","waiting":"Alice, since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"ducter-418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","waiting":"Draft","done":false}]}
